// QT Clustering Benchmark - Simplified Sequential Version
// 
// QT (Quality Threshold) clustering is an algorithm that builds clusters
// by starting with a seed point and iteratively adding the closest point
// that maintains the cluster's diameter below a threshold.

#include <algorithm>
#include <chrono>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <cstring>
#include <limits>
#include <vector>

#include "../common/results_output.hpp"

static const double MAX_WIDTH = 20.0;
static const double MAX_HEIGHT = 20.0;

// Structure to represent a point in 2D space
struct Point {
    double x, y;
};

// Structure to represent a cluster
struct Cluster {
    std::vector<int> members;
    int seed_point;
};

// Generate synthetic 2D point data in clusters
void generateSyntheticData(std::vector<Point>& points, const int N, unsigned int seed = 42) {
    auto frand = [&seed]() mutable { return rand_r(&seed) / static_cast<double>(RAND_MAX); };
    
    const double min_dim = std::min(MAX_WIDTH, MAX_HEIGHT);
    int count = 0;
    
    while (count < N) {
        // Create group_cnt points within a circle of radius R
        // around center point (cntr_x, cntr_y)
        const double cntr_x = frand() * MAX_WIDTH;
        const double cntr_y = frand() * MAX_HEIGHT;
        const double R = frand() * min_dim / 2.0;
        int group_cnt = static_cast<int>(frand() * (N / 30.0));
        
        // Make sure we don't make more points than we need
        if (group_cnt > (N - count)) {
            group_cnt = N - count;
        }
        
        while (group_cnt > 0) {
            const double sign = (frand() < 0.5) ? -1.0 : 1.0;
            const double r = frand() * R;
            const double dx = (2.0 * frand() - 1.0) * r;
            const double dy = std::sqrt(r * r - dx * dx) * sign;
            const double x = cntr_x + dx;
            const double y = cntr_y + dy;
            
            if (x < 0 || x > MAX_WIDTH || y < 0 || y > MAX_HEIGHT) {
                continue;
            }
            
            points[count] = {x, y};
            count++;
            group_cnt--;
        }
    }
}

// Calculate Euclidean distance between two points
inline double distance(const Point& p1, const Point& p2) {
    double dx = p1.x - p2.x;
    double dy = p1.y - p2.y;
    return std::sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);
}

// Find the closest unclustered point to the current cluster that maintains diameter < threshold
// Returns -1 if no such point exists
int findClosestPoint(const std::vector<int>& cluster_members,
                     const std::vector<bool>& clustered,
                     const std::vector<bool>& in_cluster,
                     const std::vector<Point>& points,
                     const double threshold,
                     const int point_count) {
    int closest_point = -1;
    double min_diameter = std::numeric_limits<double>::max();
    
    // Try each unclustered point as a candidate
    for (int candidate = 0; candidate < point_count; ++candidate) {
        // Skip if already clustered or already in this cluster
        if (clustered[candidate] || in_cluster[candidate]) continue;
        
        // Calculate the maximum distance from candidate to all cluster members
        double max_dist = 0.0;
        for (size_t i = 0; i < cluster_members.size(); ++i) {
            const int member = cluster_members[i];
            const double dist = distance(points[candidate], points[member]);
            max_dist = std::max(max_dist, dist);
        }
        
        // If adding this point keeps diameter below threshold and is better than current best
        if (max_dist < threshold && max_dist < min_diameter) {
            min_diameter = max_dist;
            closest_point = candidate;
        }
    }
    
    return closest_point;
}

// Generate a candidate cluster starting from a seed point
// Returns the cardinality (size) of the cluster
int generateCandidateCluster(const int seed_point,
                              const std::vector<bool>& clustered,
                              const std::vector<Point>& points,
                              const double threshold,
                              const int point_count,
                              std::vector<int>* cluster_members = nullptr) {
    std::vector<bool> in_cluster(point_count, false);
    std::vector<int> members;
    
    // Add seed point
    in_cluster[seed_point] = true;
    members.push_back(seed_point);
    
    // Iteratively add closest points
    while (static_cast<int>(members.size()) < point_count) {
        // Find closest point to current cluster that maintains diameter < threshold
        const int closest = findClosestPoint(members, clustered, in_cluster, points, 
                                             threshold, point_count);
        
        if (closest < 0) break; // No more points can be added
        
        in_cluster[closest] = true;
        members.push_back(closest);
    }
    
    // Copy members if requested
    if (cluster_members) {
        *cluster_members = members;
    }
    
    return static_cast<int>(members.size());
}

// Main QT clustering algorithm
std::vector<Cluster> qtClustering(const std::vector<Point>& points,
                                  const double threshold) {
    const int N = static_cast<int>(points.size());
    std::vector<bool> clustered(N, false);
    std::vector<int> unclustered_indices;
    std::vector<Cluster> clusters;
    
    // Initialize unclustered indices
    for (int i = 0; i < N; ++i) {
        unclustered_indices.push_back(i);
    }
    
    // Main clustering loop
    while (!unclustered_indices.empty()) {
        int max_cardinality = -1;
        int best_seed = -1;
        std::vector<int> best_cluster_members;
        
        // Try each unclustered point as a seed
        for (size_t i = 0; i < unclustered_indices.size(); ++i) {
            const int seed = unclustered_indices[i];
            if (clustered[seed]) continue;
            
            std::vector<int> candidate_members;
            const int cardinality = generateCandidateCluster(seed, clustered, points, 
                                                       threshold, N, 
                                                       &candidate_members);
            
            if (cardinality > max_cardinality) {
                max_cardinality = cardinality;
                best_seed = seed;
                best_cluster_members = candidate_members;
            }
        }
        
        // If we found a cluster, add it
        if (best_seed >= 0 && max_cardinality > 0) {
            Cluster cluster;
            cluster.seed_point = best_seed;
            cluster.members = best_cluster_members;
            clusters.push_back(cluster);
            
            // Mark all members as clustered
            for (size_t i = 0; i < best_cluster_members.size(); ++i) {
                clustered[best_cluster_members[i]] = true;
            }
            
            // Remove clustered points from unclustered list
            unclustered_indices.erase(
                std::remove_if(unclustered_indices.begin(), unclustered_indices.end(),
                              [&clustered](int idx) { return clustered[idx]; }),
                unclustered_indices.end()
            );
        } else {
            // No more clusters can be formed
            break;
        }
    }
    
    return clusters;
}

// Validation: check that clusters satisfy the QT clustering properties
bool validateClusters(const std::vector<Cluster>& clusters,
                     const std::vector<Point>& points,
                     const double threshold) {
    bool valid = true;
    
    printf("Validating clusters:\n");
    
    // Check each cluster
    for (size_t c = 0; c < clusters.size(); ++c) {
        const auto& cluster = clusters[c];
        double max_diameter = 0.0;
        
        // Check diameter (max distance between any two points)
        for (size_t i = 0; i < cluster.members.size(); ++i) {
            for (size_t j = i + 1; j < cluster.members.size(); ++j) {
                const double dist = distance(points[cluster.members[i]], 
                                           points[cluster.members[j]]);
                max_diameter = std::max(max_diameter, dist);
            }
        }
        
        if (c < 10) { // Print first 10 clusters
            printf("  Cluster %zu: size=%zu, seed=%d, diameter=%.4f\n", 
                   c, cluster.members.size(), cluster.seed_point, max_diameter);
        }
        
        // Validate diameter is within threshold
        if (max_diameter > threshold * 1.001) { // Allow small numerical error
            printf("ERROR: Cluster %zu has diameter %.4f > threshold %.4f\n", 
                   c, max_diameter, threshold);
            valid = false;
        }
    }
    
    // Check for duplicate memberships
    std::vector<int> membership(points.size(), -1);
    for (size_t c = 0; c < clusters.size(); ++c) {
        for (size_t i = 0; i < clusters[c].members.size(); ++i) {
            const int member = clusters[c].members[i];
            if (membership[member] >= 0) {
                printf("ERROR: Point %d appears in multiple clusters (%d and %zu)\n",
                       member, membership[member], c);
                valid = false;
            }
            membership[member] = static_cast<int>(c);
        }
    }
    
    // Count clustered points
    int clustered_count = 0;
    for (size_t i = 0; i < membership.size(); ++i) {
        if (membership[i] >= 0) clustered_count++;
    }
    
    printf("Total points: %zu, Clustered: %d, Unclustered: %zu\n",
           points.size(), clustered_count, points.size() - clustered_count);
    
    return valid;
}

void printUsage(const char* progName) {
    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
    printf("Options:\n");
    printf("  -n <num>     Number of points (default: 1000)\n");
    printf("  -t <float>   Distance threshold for clustering (default: 2.0)\n");
    printf("  -v           Enable validation\n");
    printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
    printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
}

int main(int argc, char** argv) {
    int num_points = 1000;
    double threshold = 2.0;
    bool validate = false;
    bool printResults = false;
    
    // Parse command line arguments
    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
        if (strcmp(argv[i], "-n") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            num_points = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-t") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            threshold = atof(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
            validate = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0) {
            printResults = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Unknown option: %s\n", argv[i]);
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    if (num_points <= 0 || threshold <= 0.0) {
        printf("Error: Invalid parameters (num_points=%d, threshold=%.2f)\n", 
               num_points, threshold);
        return 1;
    }
    
    printf("QT Clustering Benchmark\n");
    printf("Number of points: %d\n", num_points);
    printf("Distance threshold: %.2f\n", threshold);
    printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
    
    // Generate synthetic data
    std::vector<Point> points(num_points);
    generateSyntheticData(points, num_points);
    
    // Perform QT clustering
    auto cluster_start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    
    const std::vector<Cluster> clusters = qtClustering(points, threshold);
    
    auto cluster_end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    auto cluster_time = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(
        cluster_end - cluster_start);
    
    printf("Clustering time: %ld ms\n", cluster_time.count());
    printf("Clusters found: %zu\n", clusters.size());
    
    // Calculate statistics and performance metrics
    int total_clustered = 0;
    int max_cluster_size = 0;
    
    for (size_t i = 0; i < clusters.size(); ++i) {
        const int size = static_cast<int>(clusters[i].members.size());
        total_clustered += size;
        max_cluster_size = std::max(max_cluster_size, size);
    }
    
    const double avg_cluster_size = clusters.empty() ? 0.0 : 
        static_cast<double>(total_clustered) / clusters.size();
    
    printf("Points clustered: %d / %d (%.1f%%)\n", 
           total_clustered, num_points, 
           100.0 * total_clustered / num_points);
    printf("Average cluster size: %.2f\n", avg_cluster_size);
    printf("Maximum cluster size: %d\n", max_cluster_size);
    
    // Performance metrics
    const double time_sec = cluster_time.count() / 1000.0;
    const double clusters_per_sec = clusters.size() / time_sec;
    const double points_per_sec = num_points / time_sec;
    printf("Performance: %.1f clusters/s, %.1f points/s\n", 
           clusters_per_sec, points_per_sec);
    
    // Print results for external validation
    if (printResults) {
        // Serialize cluster membership for hashing
        std::vector<double> membershipData;
        membershipData.reserve(num_points);
        std::vector<int> membership(num_points, -1);
        for (size_t c = 0; c < clusters.size(); ++c) {
            for (size_t i = 0; i < clusters[c].members.size(); ++i) {
                membership[clusters[c].members[i]] = static_cast<int>(c);
            }
        }
        for (int m : membership) {
            membershipData.push_back(static_cast<double>(m));
        }
        print_results(membershipData, "ClusterMembership");
    }
    
    // Validation
    if (validate) {
        const bool valid = validateClusters(clusters, points, threshold);
        
        if (valid) {
            printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    return 0;
}

#pragma once

#include <algorithm>
#include <cinttypes>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <vector>

#include "hash.hpp"

// Kahan summation for numerically stable sum
template <typename T>
T kahan_sum(const std::vector<T>& data) {
    T sum = 0.0;
    T c = 0.0;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        T y = val - c;
        T t = sum + y;
        c = (t - sum) - y;
        sum = t;
    }
    return sum;
}

// Print results for floating-point arrays
template <typename T>
void print_results(const std::vector<T>& data, const char* name = "Result") {
    if (data.empty()) {
        printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
        printf("Name: %s\n", name);
        printf("Elements: 0\n");
        printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
        return;
    }

    const size_t N = data.size();
    
    // Compute statistics
    T sum = kahan_sum(data);
    T min_val = data[0];
    T max_val = data[0];
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        min_val = std::min(min_val, val);
        max_val = std::max(max_val, val);
    }
    
    // Compute hash
    hash h;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        h.add(val);
    }
    
    // Sample indices: 0, N/4, N/2, 3N/4, N-1
    size_t indices[5] = {0, N/4, N/2, 3*N/4, N-1};
    
    printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
    printf("Name: %s\n", name);
    printf("Elements: %zu\n", N);
    printf("Sum: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(sum));
    printf("Min: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(min_val));
    printf("Max: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(max_val));
    for (int i = 0; i < 5; ++i) {
        printf("Sample[%zu]: %.17e\n", indices[i], static_cast<double>(data[indices[i]]));
    }
    printf("Hash: %016" PRIx64 "\n", h.get());
    printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
}

// Print results for integer arrays (uses exact hash comparison)
template <typename T>
void print_results_int(const std::vector<T>& data, const char* name = "Result") {
    if (data.empty()) {
        printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
        printf("Name: %s\n", name);
        printf("Elements: 0\n");
        printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
        return;
    }

    const size_t N = data.size();
    
    // Compute hash
    hash h;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        h.add(val);
    }
    
    // Compute min/max
    T min_val = data[0];
    T max_val = data[0];
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        min_val = std::min(min_val, val);
        max_val = std::max(max_val, val);
    }
    
    // Sample indices: 0, N/4, N/2, 3N/4, N-1
    size_t indices[5] = {0, N/4, N/2, 3*N/4, N-1};
    
    printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
    printf("Name: %s\n", name);
    printf("Elements: %zu\n", N);
    printf("Min: %lld\n", static_cast<long long>(min_val));
    printf("Max: %lld\n", static_cast<long long>(max_val));
    for (int i = 0; i < 5; ++i) {
        printf("Sample[%zu]: %lld\n", indices[i], static_cast<long long>(data[indices[i]]));
    }
    printf("Hash: %016" PRIx64 "\n", h.get());
    printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
}

codex
The dominant cost is the repeated “closest admissible candidate” search for every seed. I’m moving that entire per-seed candidate-cluster construction to the GPU: one CUDA block owns each seed, threads scan candidate points in parallel, and a deterministic reduction preserves the original lowest-index tie breaking. The host will retain only the sequential outer choice of the largest cluster, which is required by the algorithm’s semantics.

+// the shared reduction uses (distance, point index), exactly matching the original
+// strict-improvement scan and therefore retaining deterministic tie breaking.
+__global__ void buildCandidateClusters(const Point* points, const unsigned char* clustered,
+                                       unsigned char* candidate_members, int* cardinalities,
+                                       const double threshold, const int point_count) {
+    const int seed = static_cast<int>(blockIdx.x);
+    if (seed >= point_count) return;
+
+    extern __shared__ unsigned char shared_storage[];
+    double* best_dist = reinterpret_cast<double*>(shared_storage);
+    int* best_index = reinterpret_cast<int*>(best_dist + blockDim.x);
+    unsigned char* members = candidate_members + static_cast<size_t>(seed) * point_count;
+
+    if (clustered[seed]) {
+        if (threadIdx.x == 0) cardinalities[seed] = 0;
+        return;
+    }
+
+    if (threadIdx.x == 0) {
+        for (int i = 0; i < point_count; ++i) members[i] = 0;
+        members[seed] = 1;
+        cardinalities[seed] = 1;
+    }
+    __syncthreads();
+
+    for (;;) {
+        double local_dist = CUDART_INF;
+        int local_index = point_count;
+
+        for (int candidate = threadIdx.x; candidate < point_count; candidate += blockDim.x) {
+            if (clustered[candidate] || members[candidate]) continue;
+
+            double max_dist = 0.0;
+            for (int member = 0; member < point_count; ++member) {
+                if (members[member]) {
+                    max_dist = fmax(max_dist, distance(points[candidate], points[member]));
+                }
+            }
+
+            if (max_dist < threshold &&
+                (max_dist < local_dist || (max_dist == local_dist && candidate < local_index))) {
+                local_dist = max_dist;
+                local_index = candidate;
+            }
+        }
+
+        best_dist[threadIdx.x] = local_dist;
+        best_index[threadIdx.x] = local_index;
+        __syncthreads();
+
+        for (int stride = blockDim.x / 2; stride > 0; stride >>= 1) {
+            if (threadIdx.x < stride) {
+                const double other_dist = best_dist[threadIdx.x + stride];
+                const int other_index = best_index[threadIdx.x + stride];
+                if (other_dist < best_dist[threadIdx.x] ||
+                    (other_dist == best_dist[threadIdx.x] && other_index < best_index[threadIdx.x])) {
+                    best_dist[threadIdx.x] = other_dist;
+                    best_index[threadIdx.x] = other_index;
+                }
+            }
+            __syncthreads();
         }
-        
-        // If adding this point keeps diameter below threshold and is better than current best
-        if (max_dist < threshold && max_dist < min_diameter) {
-            min_diameter = max_dist;
-            closest_point = candidate;
+
+        if (threadIdx.x == 0) {
+            const int selected = best_index[0];
+            if (selected < point_count) {
+                members[selected] = 1;
+                ++cardinalities[seed];
+            }
         }
+        __syncthreads();
+        if (best_index[0] >= point_count) break;
     }
-    
-    return closest_point;
 }

+        fprintf(stderr, "CUDA error during %s: %s\n", operation, cudaGetErrorString(error));

     }

+                (max_dist < local_dist || (max_dist == local_dist && candidate < local_index))) {
+                local_dist = max_dist;
+                local_index = candidate;
+            }
+        }
+
+        best_dist[threadIdx.x] = local_dist;
+        best_index[threadIdx.x] = local_index;
+        __syncthreads();
+
+        for (int stride = blockDim.x / 2; stride > 0; stride >>= 1) {
+            if (threadIdx.x < stride) {
+                const double other_dist = best_dist[threadIdx.x + stride];
+                const int other_index = best_index[threadIdx.x + stride];
+                if (other_dist < best_dist[threadIdx.x] ||
+                    (other_dist == best_dist[threadIdx.x] && other_index < best_index[threadIdx.x])) {
+                    best_dist[threadIdx.x] = other_dist;
+                    best_index[threadIdx.x] = other_index;
+                }
+            }
+            __syncthreads();
         }
-        
-        // If adding this point keeps diameter below threshold and is better than current best
-        if (max_dist < threshold && max_dist < min_diameter) {
-            min_diameter = max_dist;
-            closest_point = candidate;
+
+        if (threadIdx.x == 0) {
+            const int selected = best_index[0];
+            if (selected < point_count) {
+                members[selected] = 1;
+                ++cardinalities[seed];
+            }
         }
+        __syncthreads();
+        if (best_index[0] >= point_count) break;
     }

-    return closest_point;

+// One block constructs one candidate cluster. Threads scan candidates in parallel;
+// the shared reduction uses (distance, point index), exactly matching the original
+// strict-improvement scan and therefore retaining deterministic tie breaking.
+__global__ void buildCandidateClusters(const Point* points, const unsigned char* clustered,
+                                       unsigned char* candidate_members, int* cardinalities,
+                                       const double threshold, const int point_count) {
+    const int seed = static_cast<int>(blockIdx.x);
+    if (seed >= point_count) return;
+
+    extern __shared__ unsigned char shared_storage[];
+    double* best_dist = reinterpret_cast<double*>(shared_storage);
+    int* best_index = reinterpret_cast<int*>(best_dist + blockDim.x);
+    unsigned char* members = candidate_members + static_cast<size_t>(seed) * point_count;
+
+    if (clustered[seed]) {
+        if (threadIdx.x == 0) cardinalities[seed] = 0;
+        return;
+    }
+
+    if (threadIdx.x == 0) {
+        for (int i = 0; i < point_count; ++i) members[i] = 0;
+        members[seed] = 1;
+        cardinalities[seed] = 1;
+    }
+    __syncthreads();
+
+    for (;;) {
+        double local_dist = CUDART_INF;
+        int local_index = point_count;
+
+        for (int candidate = threadIdx.x; candidate < point_count; candidate += blockDim.x) {
+            if (clustered[candidate] || members[candidate]) continue;
+
+            double max_dist = 0.0;
+            for (int member = 0; member < point_count; ++member) {
+                if (members[member]) {
+                    max_dist = fmax(max_dist, distance(points[candidate], points[member]));
+                }
+            }
+
+            if (max_dist < threshold &&
+                (max_dist < local_dist || (max_dist == local_dist && candidate < local_index))) {
+                local_dist = max_dist;
+                local_index = candidate;
+            }

+        best_index[threadIdx.x] = local_index;
+        __syncthreads();
+

+                    best_index[threadIdx.x] = other_index;
+                }
+            }
+            __syncthreads();

-

+

+// the shared reduction uses (distance, point index), exactly matching the original
+// strict-improvement scan and therefore retaining deterministic tie breaking.
+__global__ void buildCandidateClusters(const Point* points, const unsigned char* clustered,
+                                       unsigned char* candidate_members, int* cardinalities,
+                                       const double threshold, const int point_count) {
+    const int seed = static_cast<int>(blockIdx.x);
+    if (seed >= point_count) return;
+
+    extern __shared__ unsigned char shared_storage[];
+    double* best_dist = reinterpret_cast<double*>(shared_storage);
+    int* best_index = reinterpret_cast<int*>(best_dist + blockDim.x);
+    unsigned char* members = candidate_members + static_cast<size_t>(seed) * point_count;
+
+    if (clustered[seed]) {
+        if (threadIdx.x == 0) cardinalities[seed] = 0;
+        return;
+    }
+
+    if (threadIdx.x == 0) {
+        for (int i = 0; i < point_count; ++i) members[i] = 0;
+        members[seed] = 1;
+        cardinalities[seed] = 1;
+    }
+    __syncthreads();
+
+    for (;;) {
+        double local_dist = CUDART_INF;
+        int local_index = point_count;
+
+        for (int candidate = threadIdx.x; candidate < point_count; candidate += blockDim.x) {
+            if (clustered[candidate] || members[candidate]) continue;
+
+            double max_dist = 0.0;
+            for (int member = 0; member < point_count; ++member) {
+                if (members[member]) {
+                    max_dist = fmax(max_dist, distance(points[candidate], points[member]));
+                }
+            }
+
+            if (max_dist < threshold &&
+                (max_dist < local_dist || (max_dist == local_dist && candidate < local_index))) {
+                local_dist = max_dist;
+                local_index = candidate;
+            }
+        }
+
+        best_dist[threadIdx.x] = local_dist;
+        best_index[threadIdx.x] = local_index;
+        __syncthreads();
+
+        for (int stride = blockDim.x / 2; stride > 0; stride >>= 1) {
+            if (threadIdx.x < stride) {
+                const double other_dist = best_dist[threadIdx.x + stride];
+                const int other_index = best_index[threadIdx.x + stride];
+                if (other_dist < best_dist[threadIdx.x] ||
+                    (other_dist == best_dist[threadIdx.x] && other_index < best_index[threadIdx.x])) {
+                    best_dist[threadIdx.x] = other_dist;
+                    best_index[threadIdx.x] = other_index;
+                }
+            }

-

+

Compilation reached the CUDA kernel and exposed one toolkit-specific constant issue, not an algorithmic problem. I’m correcting that and pinning the release build to the available RTX 3090 architecture so the generated code uses the GPU’s native instruction set without an empty-architecture warning.

-        if (clustered[candidate] || in_cluster[candidate]) continue;
-        
-        // Calculate the maximum distance from candidate to all cluster members
-        double max_dist = 0.0;
-        for (size_t i = 0; i < cluster_members.size(); ++i) {
-            const int member = cluster_members[i];
-            const double dist = distance(points[candidate], points[member]);
-            max_dist = std::max(max_dist, dist);
+// One block constructs one candidate cluster. Threads scan candidates in parallel;
+// the shared reduction uses (distance, point index), exactly matching the original
+// strict-improvement scan and therefore retaining deterministic tie breaking.
+__global__ void buildCandidateClusters(const Point* points, const unsigned char* clustered,
+                                       unsigned char* candidate_members, int* cardinalities,
+                                       const double threshold, const int point_count) {
+    const int seed = static_cast<int>(blockIdx.x);
+    if (seed >= point_count) return;
+
+    extern __shared__ unsigned char shared_storage[];
+    double* best_dist = reinterpret_cast<double*>(shared_storage);
+    int* best_index = reinterpret_cast<int*>(best_dist + blockDim.x);
+    unsigned char* members = candidate_members + static_cast<size_t>(seed) * point_count;
+
+    if (clustered[seed]) {
+        if (threadIdx.x == 0) cardinalities[seed] = 0;
+        return;
+    }
+
+    if (threadIdx.x == 0) {
+        for (int i = 0; i < point_count; ++i) members[i] = 0;
+        members[seed] = 1;
+        cardinalities[seed] = 1;
+    }
+    __syncthreads();
+
+    for (;;) {
+        double local_dist = 1.7976931348623157e+308;
+        int local_index = point_count;
+
+        for (int candidate = threadIdx.x; candidate < point_count; candidate += blockDim.x) {
+            if (clustered[candidate] || members[candidate]) continue;
+
+            double max_dist = 0.0;
+            for (int member = 0; member < point_count; ++member) {
+                if (members[member]) {
+                    max_dist = fmax(max_dist, distance(points[candidate], points[member]));
+                }
+            }
+
+            if (max_dist < threshold &&
+                (max_dist < local_dist || (max_dist == local_dist && candidate < local_index))) {
+                local_dist = max_dist;
+                local_index = candidate;
+            }
+        }
+
+        best_dist[threadIdx.x] = local_dist;
+        best_index[threadIdx.x] = local_index;
+        __syncthreads();
+
+        for (int stride = blockDim.x / 2; stride > 0; stride >>= 1) {
+            if (threadIdx.x < stride) {
+                const double other_dist = best_dist[threadIdx.x + stride];
+                const int other_index = best_index[threadIdx.x + stride];
+                if (other_dist < best_dist[threadIdx.x] ||
+                    (other_dist == best_dist[threadIdx.x] && other_index < best_index[threadIdx.x])) {
+                    best_dist[threadIdx.x] = other_dist;
+                    best_index[threadIdx.x] = other_index;
+                }
+            }
+            __syncthreads();
         }
-        
-        // If adding this point keeps diameter below threshold and is better than current best
-        if (max_dist < threshold && max_dist < min_diameter) {
-            min_diameter = max_dist;
-            closest_point = candidate;
+
+        if (threadIdx.x == 0) {
+            const int selected = best_index[0];
+            if (selected < point_count) {
+                members[selected] = 1;
+                ++cardinalities[seed];
+            }
         }
+        __syncthreads();
+        if (best_index[0] >= point_count) break;
     }
-    
-    return closest_point;
 }

-    for (int candidate = 0; candidate < point_count; ++candidate) {
-        // Skip if already clustered or already in this cluster
-        if (clustered[candidate] || in_cluster[candidate]) continue;
-        
-        // Calculate the maximum distance from candidate to all cluster members
-        double max_dist = 0.0;
-        for (size_t i = 0; i < cluster_members.size(); ++i) {
-            const int member = cluster_members[i];
-            const double dist = distance(points[candidate], points[member]);
-            max_dist = std::max(max_dist, dist);
+// One block constructs one candidate cluster. Threads scan candidates in parallel;
+// the shared reduction uses (distance, point index), exactly matching the original
+// strict-improvement scan and therefore retaining deterministic tie breaking.
+__global__ void buildCandidateClusters(const Point* points, const unsigned char* clustered,
+                                       unsigned char* candidate_members, int* cardinalities,
+                                       const double threshold, const int point_count) {
+    const int seed = static_cast<int>(blockIdx.x);
+    if (seed >= point_count) return;
+
+    extern __shared__ unsigned char shared_storage[];
+    double* best_dist = reinterpret_cast<double*>(shared_storage);
+    int* best_index = reinterpret_cast<int*>(best_dist + blockDim.x);
+    unsigned char* members = candidate_members + static_cast<size_t>(seed) * point_count;
+
+    if (clustered[seed]) {
+        if (threadIdx.x == 0) cardinalities[seed] = 0;
+        return;
+    }
+
+    if (threadIdx.x == 0) {
+        for (int i = 0; i < point_count; ++i) members[i] = 0;
+        members[seed] = 1;
+        cardinalities[seed] = 1;
+    }
+    __syncthreads();
+
+    for (;;) {
+        double local_dist = 1.7976931348623157e+308;
+        int local_index = point_count;
+
+        for (int candidate = threadIdx.x; candidate < point_count; candidate += blockDim.x) {
+            if (clustered[candidate] || members[candidate]) continue;
+
+            double max_dist = 0.0;
+            for (int member = 0; member < point_count; ++member) {
+                if (members[member]) {
+                    max_dist = fmax(max_dist, distance(points[candidate], points[member]));
+                }
+            }
+
+            if (max_dist < threshold &&
+                (max_dist < local_dist || (max_dist == local_dist && candidate < local_index))) {
+                local_dist = max_dist;
+                local_index = candidate;
+            }
+        }
+
+        best_dist[threadIdx.x] = local_dist;
+        best_index[threadIdx.x] = local_index;
+        __syncthreads();
+
+        for (int stride = blockDim.x / 2; stride > 0; stride >>= 1) {
+            if (threadIdx.x < stride) {
+                const double other_dist = best_dist[threadIdx.x + stride];
+                const int other_index = best_index[threadIdx.x + stride];
+                if (other_dist < best_dist[threadIdx.x] ||
+                    (other_dist == best_dist[threadIdx.x] && other_index < best_index[threadIdx.x])) {
+                    best_dist[threadIdx.x] = other_dist;
+                    best_index[threadIdx.x] = other_index;
+                }
+            }
+            __syncthreads();
         }
-        
-        // If adding this point keeps diameter below threshold and is better than current best
-        if (max_dist < threshold && max_dist < min_diameter) {
-            min_diameter = max_dist;
-            closest_point = candidate;
+
+        if (threadIdx.x == 0) {
+            const int selected = best_index[0];
+            if (selected < point_count) {
+                members[selected] = 1;
+                ++cardinalities[seed];
+            }
         }
+        __syncthreads();
+        if (best_index[0] >= point_count) break;
     }
-    
-    return closest_point;
 }

+__host__ __device__ inline double distance(const Point& p1, const Point& p2) {

+// One block constructs one candidate cluster. Threads scan candidates in parallel;
+// the shared reduction uses (distance, point index), exactly matching the original
+// strict-improvement scan and therefore retaining deterministic tie breaking.
+__global__ void buildCandidateClusters(const Point* points, const unsigned char* clustered,
+                                       unsigned char* candidate_members, int* cardinalities,
+                                       const double threshold, const int point_count) {
+    const int seed = static_cast<int>(blockIdx.x);
+    if (seed >= point_count) return;
+
+    extern __shared__ unsigned char shared_storage[];
+    double* best_dist = reinterpret_cast<double*>(shared_storage);
+    int* best_index = reinterpret_cast<int*>(best_dist + blockDim.x);
+    unsigned char* members = candidate_members + static_cast<size_t>(seed) * point_count;
+
+    if (clustered[seed]) {
+        if (threadIdx.x == 0) cardinalities[seed] = 0;
+        return;
+    }
+
+    if (threadIdx.x == 0) {
+        for (int i = 0; i < point_count; ++i) members[i] = 0;
+        members[seed] = 1;
+        cardinalities[seed] = 1;
+    }
+    __syncthreads();
+
+    for (;;) {
+        double local_dist = 1.7976931348623157e+308;
+        int local_index = point_count;
+
+        for (int candidate = threadIdx.x; candidate < point_count; candidate += blockDim.x) {
+            if (clustered[candidate] || members[candidate]) continue;
+
+            double max_dist = 0.0;
+            for (int member = 0; member < point_count; ++member) {
+                if (members[member]) {
+                    max_dist = fmax(max_dist, distance(points[candidate], points[member]));
+                }
+            }
+
+            if (max_dist < threshold &&
+                (max_dist < local_dist || (max_dist == local_dist && candidate < local_index))) {
+                local_dist = max_dist;
+                local_index = candidate;
+            }
+        }
+
+        best_dist[threadIdx.x] = local_dist;
+        best_index[threadIdx.x] = local_index;
+        __syncthreads();
+
+        for (int stride = blockDim.x / 2; stride > 0; stride >>= 1) {
+            if (threadIdx.x < stride) {
+                const double other_dist = best_dist[threadIdx.x + stride];
+                const int other_index = best_index[threadIdx.x + stride];
+                if (other_dist < best_dist[threadIdx.x] ||
+                    (other_dist == best_dist[threadIdx.x] && other_index < best_index[threadIdx.x])) {
+                    best_dist[threadIdx.x] = other_dist;
+                    best_index[threadIdx.x] = other_index;
+                }
+            }
+            __syncthreads();
         }
-        
-        // If adding this point keeps diameter below threshold and is better than current best
-        if (max_dist < threshold && max_dist < min_diameter) {
-            min_diameter = max_dist;
-            closest_point = candidate;
+
+        if (threadIdx.x == 0) {
+            const int selected = best_index[0];
+            if (selected < point_count) {
+                members[selected] = 1;
+                ++cardinalities[seed];
+            }
         }
+        __syncthreads();
+        if (best_index[0] >= point_count) break;
     }
-    
-    return closest_point;
 }

 // Calculate Euclidean distance between two points
-inline double distance(const Point& p1, const Point& p2) {
+__host__ __device__ inline double distance(const Point& p1, const Point& p2) {
     double dx = p1.x - p2.x;
     double dy = p1.y - p2.y;
     return std::sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);

+// One block constructs one candidate cluster. Threads scan candidates in parallel;
+// the shared reduction uses (distance, point index), exactly matching the original
+// strict-improvement scan and therefore retaining deterministic tie breaking.
+__global__ void buildCandidateClusters(const Point* points, const unsigned char* clustered,
+                                       unsigned char* candidate_members, int* cardinalities,
+                                       const double threshold, const int point_count) {
+    const int seed = static_cast<int>(blockIdx.x);
+    if (seed >= point_count) return;
+
+    extern __shared__ unsigned char shared_storage[];
+    double* best_dist = reinterpret_cast<double*>(shared_storage);
+    int* best_index = reinterpret_cast<int*>(best_dist + blockDim.x);
+    unsigned char* members = candidate_members + static_cast<size_t>(seed) * point_count;
+
+    if (clustered[seed]) {
+        if (threadIdx.x == 0) cardinalities[seed] = 0;
+        return;
+    }
+
+    if (threadIdx.x == 0) {
+        for (int i = 0; i < point_count; ++i) members[i] = 0;
+        members[seed] = 1;
+        cardinalities[seed] = 1;
+    }
+    __syncthreads();
+
+    for (;;) {
+        double local_dist = 1.7976931348623157e+308;
+        int local_index = point_count;
+
+        for (int candidate = threadIdx.x; candidate < point_count; candidate += blockDim.x) {
+            if (clustered[candidate] || members[candidate]) continue;
+
+            double max_dist = 0.0;
+            for (int member = 0; member < point_count; ++member) {
+                if (members[member]) {
+                    max_dist = fmax(max_dist, distance(points[candidate], points[member]));
+                }
+            }
+
+            if (max_dist < threshold &&
+                (max_dist < local_dist || (max_dist == local_dist && candidate < local_index))) {
+                local_dist = max_dist;
+                local_index = candidate;
+            }
+        }
+
+        best_dist[threadIdx.x] = local_dist;
+        best_index[threadIdx.x] = local_index;
+        __syncthreads();
+
+        for (int stride = blockDim.x / 2; stride > 0; stride >>= 1) {
+            if (threadIdx.x < stride) {
+                const double other_dist = best_dist[threadIdx.x + stride];
+                const int other_index = best_index[threadIdx.x + stride];
+                if (other_dist < best_dist[threadIdx.x] ||
+                    (other_dist == best_dist[threadIdx.x] && other_index < best_index[threadIdx.x])) {
+                    best_dist[threadIdx.x] = other_dist;
+                    best_index[threadIdx.x] = other_index;
+                }
+            }
+            __syncthreads();
         }
-        
-        // If adding this point keeps diameter below threshold and is better than current best
-        if (max_dist < threshold && max_dist < min_diameter) {
-            min_diameter = max_dist;
-            closest_point = candidate;
+
+        if (threadIdx.x == 0) {
+            const int selected = best_index[0];
+            if (selected < point_count) {
+                members[selected] = 1;
+                ++cardinalities[seed];
+            }
         }
+        __syncthreads();
+        if (best_index[0] >= point_count) break;
     }
-    
-    return closest_point;
 }

-inline double distance(const Point& p1, const Point& p2) {
+__host__ __device__ inline double distance(const Point& p1, const Point& p2) {
     double dx = p1.x - p2.x;
     double dy = p1.y - p2.y;
     return std::sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);
 }

-

+// One block constructs one candidate cluster. Threads scan candidates in parallel;
+// the shared reduction uses (distance, point index), exactly matching the original
+// strict-improvement scan and therefore retaining deterministic tie breaking.
+__global__ void buildCandidateClusters(const Point* points, const unsigned char* clustered,
+                                       unsigned char* candidate_members, int* cardinalities,
+                                       const double threshold, const int point_count) {
+    const int seed = static_cast<int>(blockIdx.x);
+    if (seed >= point_count) return;
+
+    extern __shared__ unsigned char shared_storage[];
+    double* best_dist = reinterpret_cast<double*>(shared_storage);
+    int* best_index = reinterpret_cast<int*>(best_dist + blockDim.x);
+    unsigned char* members = candidate_members + static_cast<size_t>(seed) * point_count;
+
+    if (clustered[seed]) {
+        if (threadIdx.x == 0) cardinalities[seed] = 0;
+        return;
+    }
+
+    if (threadIdx.x == 0) {
+        for (int i = 0; i < point_count; ++i) members[i] = 0;
+        members[seed] = 1;
+        cardinalities[seed] = 1;
+    }
+    __syncthreads();
+
+    for (;;) {
+        double local_dist = 1.7976931348623157e+308;
+        int local_index = point_count;
+
+        for (int candidate = threadIdx.x; candidate < point_count; candidate += blockDim.x) {
+            if (clustered[candidate] || members[candidate]) continue;
+
+            double max_dist = 0.0;
+            for (int member = 0; member < point_count; ++member) {
+                if (members[member]) {
+                    max_dist = fmax(max_dist, distance(points[candidate], points[member]));
+                }
+            }
+
+            if (max_dist < threshold &&
+                (max_dist < local_dist || (max_dist == local_dist && candidate < local_index))) {
+                local_dist = max_dist;
+                local_index = candidate;
+            }
+        }
+
+        best_dist[threadIdx.x] = local_dist;
+        best_index[threadIdx.x] = local_index;
+        __syncthreads();
+
+        for (int stride = blockDim.x / 2; stride > 0; stride >>= 1) {
+            if (threadIdx.x < stride) {
+                const double other_dist = best_dist[threadIdx.x + stride];
+                const int other_index = best_index[threadIdx.x + stride];
+                if (other_dist < best_dist[threadIdx.x] ||
+                    (other_dist == best_dist[threadIdx.x] && other_index < best_index[threadIdx.x])) {
+                    best_dist[threadIdx.x] = other_dist;
+                    best_index[threadIdx.x] = other_index;
+                }
+            }
+            __syncthreads();
         }
-        
-        // If adding this point keeps diameter below threshold and is better than current best
-        if (max_dist < threshold && max_dist < min_diameter) {
-            min_diameter = max_dist;
-            closest_point = candidate;
+
+        if (threadIdx.x == 0) {
+            const int selected = best_index[0];
+            if (selected < point_count) {
+                members[selected] = 1;
+                ++cardinalities[seed];
+            }
         }
+        __syncthreads();
+        if (best_index[0] >= point_count) break;
     }
-    
-    return closest_point;
 }

codex
Validation passes for both edge cases (all singleton clusters and one cluster containing every point). I’m making one further performance improvement before finalizing: pairwise distances are invariant across all outer QT iterations, so I’ll compute them once on the GPU and reuse them instead of recomputing the same square roots inside every candidate search.

+__host__ __device__ inline double distance(const Point& p1, const Point& p2) {
     double dx = p1.x - p2.x;
     double dy = p1.y - p2.y;
     return std::sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);

+__global__ void buildDistanceMatrix(const Point* points, double* distances, const int point_count) {
+    const size_t index = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    const size_t total = static_cast<size_t>(point_count) * point_count;
+    if (index >= total) return;
+    const int candidate = static_cast<int>(index / point_count);
+    const int member = static_cast<int>(index % point_count);
+    distances[index] = distance(points[candidate], points[member]);
+}
+
+// One block constructs one candidate cluster. Threads scan candidates in parallel;
+// the shared reduction uses (distance, point index), exactly matching the original
+// strict-improvement scan and therefore retaining deterministic tie breaking.
+__global__ void buildCandidateClusters(const Point* points, const unsigned char* clustered,
+                                       unsigned char* candidate_members, int* cardinalities,
+                                       const double* distances,
+                                       const double threshold, const int point_count) {
+    const int seed = static_cast<int>(blockIdx.x);
+    if (seed >= point_count) return;
+
+    extern __shared__ unsigned char shared_storage[];
+    double* best_dist = reinterpret_cast<double*>(shared_storage);
+    int* best_index = reinterpret_cast<int*>(best_dist + blockDim.x);
+    unsigned char* members = candidate_members + static_cast<size_t>(seed) * point_count;
+
+    if (clustered[seed]) {
+        if (threadIdx.x == 0) cardinalities[seed] = 0;
+        return;
+    }
+
+    if (threadIdx.x == 0) {
+        for (int i = 0; i < point_count; ++i) members[i] = 0;
+        members[seed] = 1;
+        cardinalities[seed] = 1;
+    }
+    __syncthreads();
+
+    for (;;) {
+        double local_dist = 1.7976931348623157e+308;
+        int local_index = point_count;
+
+        for (int candidate = threadIdx.x; candidate < point_count; candidate += blockDim.x) {
+            if (clustered[candidate] || members[candidate]) continue;
+
+            double max_dist = 0.0;
+            for (int member = 0; member < point_count; ++member) {
+                if (members[member]) {
+                    max_dist = fmax(max_dist, distances[static_cast<size_t>(candidate) * point_count + member]);
+                }
+            }
+
+            if (max_dist < threshold &&
+                (max_dist < local_dist || (max_dist == local_dist && candidate < local_index))) {
+                local_dist = max_dist;
+                local_index = candidate;
+            }
+        }
+
+        best_dist[threadIdx.x] = local_dist;
+        best_index[threadIdx.x] = local_index;
+        __syncthreads();
+
+        for (int stride = blockDim.x / 2; stride > 0; stride >>= 1) {
+            if (threadIdx.x < stride) {
+                const double other_dist = best_dist[threadIdx.x + stride];
+                const int other_index = best_index[threadIdx.x + stride];
+                if (other_dist < best_dist[threadIdx.x] ||
+                    (other_dist == best_dist[threadIdx.x] && other_index < best_index[threadIdx.x])) {
+                    best_dist[threadIdx.x] = other_dist;
+                    best_index[threadIdx.x] = other_index;
+                }
+            }
+            __syncthreads();
         }
-        
-        // If adding this point keeps diameter below threshold and is better than current best
-        if (max_dist < threshold && max_dist < min_diameter) {
-            min_diameter = max_dist;
-            closest_point = candidate;
+
+        if (threadIdx.x == 0) {
+            const int selected = best_index[0];
+            if (selected < point_count) {
+                members[selected] = 1;
+                ++cardinalities[seed];
+            }
         }
+        __syncthreads();
+        if (best_index[0] >= point_count) break;
     }
-    
-    return closest_point;
 }

+    double* best_dist = reinterpret_cast<double*>(shared_storage);
+    int* best_index = reinterpret_cast<int*>(best_dist + blockDim.x);
+    unsigned char* members = candidate_members + static_cast<size_t>(seed) * point_count;
+
+    if (clustered[seed]) {
+        if (threadIdx.x == 0) cardinalities[seed] = 0;
+        return;
+    }
+
+    if (threadIdx.x == 0) {
+        for (int i = 0; i < point_count; ++i) members[i] = 0;
+        members[seed] = 1;
+        cardinalities[seed] = 1;
+    }
+    __syncthreads();
+
+    for (;;) {
+        double local_dist = 1.7976931348623157e+308;
+        int local_index = point_count;
+
+        for (int candidate = threadIdx.x; candidate < point_count; candidate += blockDim.x) {
+            if (clustered[candidate] || members[candidate]) continue;
+
+            double max_dist = 0.0;
+            for (int member = 0; member < point_count; ++member) {
+                if (members[member]) {
+                    max_dist = fmax(max_dist, distances[static_cast<size_t>(candidate) * point_count + member]);
+                }
+            }
+
+            if (max_dist < threshold &&
+                (max_dist < local_dist || (max_dist == local_dist && candidate < local_index))) {
+                local_dist = max_dist;
+                local_index = candidate;
+            }
+        }
+
+        best_dist[threadIdx.x] = local_dist;
+        best_index[threadIdx.x] = local_index;
+        __syncthreads();
+
+        for (int stride = blockDim.x / 2; stride > 0; stride >>= 1) {
+            if (threadIdx.x < stride) {
+                const double other_dist = best_dist[threadIdx.x + stride];
+                const int other_index = best_index[threadIdx.x + stride];
+                if (other_dist < best_dist[threadIdx.x] ||
+                    (other_dist == best_dist[threadIdx.x] && other_index < best_index[threadIdx.x])) {
+                    best_dist[threadIdx.x] = other_dist;
+                    best_index[threadIdx.x] = other_index;
+                }
+            }
+            __syncthreads();
         }
-        
-        // If adding this point keeps diameter below threshold and is better than current best
-        if (max_dist < threshold && max_dist < min_diameter) {
-            min_diameter = max_dist;
-            closest_point = candidate;
+
+        if (threadIdx.x == 0) {
+            const int selected = best_index[0];
+            if (selected < point_count) {
+                members[selected] = 1;
+                ++cardinalities[seed];
+            }
         }
+        __syncthreads();
+        if (best_index[0] >= point_count) break;
     }

-    return closest_point;

+    const size_t index = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    const size_t total = static_cast<size_t>(point_count) * point_count;
+    if (index >= total) return;
+    const int candidate = static_cast<int>(index / point_count);

 // Calculate Euclidean distance between two points
-inline double distance(const Point& p1, const Point& p2) {
+__host__ __device__ inline double distance(const Point& p1, const Point& p2) {
     double dx = p1.x - p2.x;
     double dy = p1.y - p2.y;
     return std::sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);
 }

-int findClosestPoint(const std::vector<int>& cluster_members,

-    
-    // Try each unclustered point as a candidate
-    for (int candidate = 0; candidate < point_count; ++candidate) {
-        // Skip if already clustered or already in this cluster
-        if (clustered[candidate] || in_cluster[candidate]) continue;
-

+__global__ void buildDistanceMatrix(const Point* points, double* distances, const int point_count) {
+    const size_t index = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    const size_t total = static_cast<size_t>(point_count) * point_count;
+    if (index >= total) return;
+    const int candidate = static_cast<int>(index / point_count);
+    const int member = static_cast<int>(index % point_count);
+    distances[index] = distance(points[candidate], points[member]);
+}
+
+// One block constructs one candidate cluster. Threads scan candidates in parallel;
+// the shared reduction uses (distance, point index), exactly matching the original
+// strict-improvement scan and therefore retaining deterministic tie breaking.
+__global__ void buildCandidateClusters(const unsigned char* clustered,
+                                       unsigned char* candidate_members, int* cardinalities,
+                                       const double* distances,
+                                       const double threshold, const int point_count) {
+    const int seed = static_cast<int>(blockIdx.x);
+    if (seed >= point_count) return;
+
+    extern __shared__ unsigned char shared_storage[];
+    double* best_dist = reinterpret_cast<double*>(shared_storage);
+    int* best_index = reinterpret_cast<int*>(best_dist + blockDim.x);
+    unsigned char* members = candidate_members + static_cast<size_t>(seed) * point_count;
+
+    if (clustered[seed]) {
+        if (threadIdx.x == 0) cardinalities[seed] = 0;
+        return;
+    }
+
+    if (threadIdx.x == 0) {
+        for (int i = 0; i < point_count; ++i) members[i] = 0;
+        members[seed] = 1;
+        cardinalities[seed] = 1;
+    }
+    __syncthreads();
+
+    for (;;) {
+        double local_dist = 1.7976931348623157e+308;
+        int local_index = point_count;
+
+        for (int candidate = threadIdx.x; candidate < point_count; candidate += blockDim.x) {
+            if (clustered[candidate] || members[candidate]) continue;
+
+            double max_dist = 0.0;
+            for (int member = 0; member < point_count; ++member) {
+                if (members[member]) {
+                    max_dist = fmax(max_dist, distances[static_cast<size_t>(candidate) * point_count + member]);
+                }
+            }
+
+            if (max_dist < threshold &&
+                (max_dist < local_dist || (max_dist == local_dist && candidate < local_index))) {
+                local_dist = max_dist;
+                local_index = candidate;
+            }
+        }
+
+        best_dist[threadIdx.x] = local_dist;
+        best_index[threadIdx.x] = local_index;
+        __syncthreads();
+
+        for (int stride = blockDim.x / 2; stride > 0; stride >>= 1) {
+            if (threadIdx.x < stride) {
+                const double other_dist = best_dist[threadIdx.x + stride];
+                const int other_index = best_index[threadIdx.x + stride];
+                if (other_dist < best_dist[threadIdx.x] ||
+                    (other_dist == best_dist[threadIdx.x] && other_index < best_index[threadIdx.x])) {
+                    best_dist[threadIdx.x] = other_dist;
+                    best_index[threadIdx.x] = other_index;
+                }
+            }
+            __syncthreads();
         }
-        
-        // If adding this point keeps diameter below threshold and is better than current best
-        if (max_dist < threshold && max_dist < min_diameter) {
-            min_diameter = max_dist;
-            closest_point = candidate;
+
+        if (threadIdx.x == 0) {
+            const int selected = best_index[0];
+            if (selected < point_count) {
+                members[selected] = 1;
+                ++cardinalities[seed];
+            }
         }
+        __syncthreads();
+        if (best_index[0] >= point_count) break;
     }
-    
-    return closest_point;
 }

+__global__ void buildDistanceMatrix(const Point* points, double* distances, const int point_count) {
+    const size_t index = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    const size_t total = static_cast<size_t>(point_count) * point_count;
+    if (index >= total) return;
+    const int candidate = static_cast<int>(index / point_count);
+    const int member = static_cast<int>(index % point_count);
+    distances[index] = distance(points[candidate], points[member]);
+}
+
+// One block constructs one candidate cluster. Threads scan candidates in parallel;
+// the shared reduction uses (distance, point index), exactly matching the original
+// strict-improvement scan and therefore retaining deterministic tie breaking.
+__global__ void buildCandidateClusters(const unsigned char* clustered,
+                                       unsigned char* candidate_members, int* cardinalities,
+                                       const double* distances,
+                                       const double threshold, const int point_count) {
+    const int seed = static_cast<int>(blockIdx.x);
+    if (seed >= point_count) return;
+
+    extern __shared__ unsigned char shared_storage[];
+    double* best_dist = reinterpret_cast<double*>(shared_storage);
+    int* best_index = reinterpret_cast<int*>(best_dist + blockDim.x);
+    unsigned char* members = candidate_members + static_cast<size_t>(seed) * point_count;
+
+    if (clustered[seed]) {
+        if (threadIdx.x == 0) cardinalities[seed] = 0;
+        return;
+    }
+
+    if (threadIdx.x == 0) {
+        for (int i = 0; i < point_count; ++i) members[i] = 0;
+        members[seed] = 1;
+        cardinalities[seed] = 1;
+    }
+    __syncthreads();
+
+    for (;;) {
+        double local_dist = 1.7976931348623157e+308;
+        int local_index = point_count;
+
+        for (int candidate = threadIdx.x; candidate < point_count; candidate += blockDim.x) {
+            if (clustered[candidate] || members[candidate]) continue;
+
+            double max_dist = 0.0;
+            for (int member = 0; member < point_count; ++member) {
+                if (members[member]) {
+                    max_dist = fmax(max_dist, distances[static_cast<size_t>(candidate) * point_count + member]);
+                }
+            }
+
+            if (max_dist < threshold &&
+                (max_dist < local_dist || (max_dist == local_dist && candidate < local_index))) {
+                local_dist = max_dist;
+                local_index = candidate;
+            }
+        }
+
+        best_dist[threadIdx.x] = local_dist;
+        best_index[threadIdx.x] = local_index;
+        __syncthreads();
+
+        for (int stride = blockDim.x / 2; stride > 0; stride >>= 1) {
+            if (threadIdx.x < stride) {
+                const double other_dist = best_dist[threadIdx.x + stride];
+                const int other_index = best_index[threadIdx.x + stride];
+                if (other_dist < best_dist[threadIdx.x] ||
+                    (other_dist == best_dist[threadIdx.x] && other_index < best_index[threadIdx.x])) {
+                    best_dist[threadIdx.x] = other_dist;
+                    best_index[threadIdx.x] = other_index;
+                }
+            }
+            __syncthreads();
         }
-        
-        // If adding this point keeps diameter below threshold and is better than current best
-        if (max_dist < threshold && max_dist < min_diameter) {
-            min_diameter = max_dist;
-            closest_point = candidate;
+
+        if (threadIdx.x == 0) {
+            const int selected = best_index[0];
+            if (selected < point_count) {
+                members[selected] = 1;
+                ++cardinalities[seed];
+            }
         }
+        __syncthreads();
+        if (best_index[0] >= point_count) break;
     }
-    
-    return closest_point;

+    double* device_distances = nullptr;
+    cudaCheck(cudaMalloc(&device_points, sizeof(Point) * N), "allocating points");

+__host__ __device__ inline double distance(const Point& p1, const Point& p2) {
     double dx = p1.x - p2.x;
     double dy = p1.y - p2.y;
     return std::sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);

+__global__ void buildDistanceMatrix(const Point* points, double* distances, const int point_count) {
+    const size_t index = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    const size_t total = static_cast<size_t>(point_count) * point_count;
+    if (index >= total) return;
+    const int candidate = static_cast<int>(index / point_count);
+    const int member = static_cast<int>(index % point_count);
+    distances[index] = distance(points[candidate], points[member]);
+}
+
+// One block constructs one candidate cluster. Threads scan candidates in parallel;
+// the shared reduction uses (distance, point index), exactly matching the original
+// strict-improvement scan and therefore retaining deterministic tie breaking.
+__global__ void buildCandidateClusters(const unsigned char* clustered,
+                                       unsigned char* candidate_members, int* cardinalities,
+                                       const double* distances,
+                                       const double threshold, const int point_count) {
+    const int seed = static_cast<int>(blockIdx.x);
+    if (seed >= point_count) return;
+
+    extern __shared__ unsigned char shared_storage[];
+    double* best_dist = reinterpret_cast<double*>(shared_storage);
+    int* best_index = reinterpret_cast<int*>(best_dist + blockDim.x);
+    unsigned char* members = candidate_members + static_cast<size_t>(seed) * point_count;
+
+    if (clustered[seed]) {
+        if (threadIdx.x == 0) cardinalities[seed] = 0;
+        return;
+    }
+
+    if (threadIdx.x == 0) {
+        for (int i = 0; i < point_count; ++i) members[i] = 0;
+        members[seed] = 1;
+        cardinalities[seed] = 1;
+    }
+    __syncthreads();
+
+    for (;;) {
+        double local_dist = 1.7976931348623157e+308;
+        int local_index = point_count;
+
+        for (int candidate = threadIdx.x; candidate < point_count; candidate += blockDim.x) {
+            if (clustered[candidate] || members[candidate]) continue;
+
+            double max_dist = 0.0;
+            for (int member = 0; member < point_count; ++member) {
+                if (members[member]) {
+                    max_dist = fmax(max_dist, distances[static_cast<size_t>(candidate) * point_count + member]);
+                }
+            }
+
+            if (max_dist < threshold &&
+                (max_dist < local_dist || (max_dist == local_dist && candidate < local_index))) {
+                local_dist = max_dist;
+                local_index = candidate;
+            }
+        }
+
+        best_dist[threadIdx.x] = local_dist;
+        best_index[threadIdx.x] = local_index;
+        __syncthreads();
+
+        for (int stride = blockDim.x / 2; stride > 0; stride >>= 1) {
+            if (threadIdx.x < stride) {
+                const double other_dist = best_dist[threadIdx.x + stride];
+                const int other_index = best_index[threadIdx.x + stride];
+                if (other_dist < best_dist[threadIdx.x] ||
+                    (other_dist == best_dist[threadIdx.x] && other_index < best_index[threadIdx.x])) {
+                    best_dist[threadIdx.x] = other_dist;
+                    best_index[threadIdx.x] = other_index;
+                }
+            }
+            __syncthreads();
         }
-        
-        // If adding this point keeps diameter below threshold and is better than current best
-        if (max_dist < threshold && max_dist < min_diameter) {
-            min_diameter = max_dist;
-            closest_point = candidate;
+
+        if (threadIdx.x == 0) {
+            const int selected = best_index[0];
+            if (selected < point_count) {
+                members[selected] = 1;
+                ++cardinalities[seed];
+            }
         }
+        __syncthreads();
+        if (best_index[0] >= point_count) break;
     }
-    
-    return closest_point;
 }

codex
The distance-cache experiment preserved the exact results but measured slower at the benchmark sizes because each block’s access pattern becomes memory-latency bound. I’m removing that cache and keeping the arithmetic kernel, which is faster on the installed GPU for this workload; the main CUDA parallelization remains unchanged.

__global__ void buildDistanceMatrix(const Point* points, double* distances, const int point_count) {
    const size_t index = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
    const size_t total = static_cast<size_t>(point_count) * point_count;
    if (index >= total) return;
    const int candidate = static_cast<int>(index / point_count);
    const int member = static_cast<int>(index % point_count);
    distances[index] = distance(points[candidate], points[member]);
}

+__global__ void buildCandidateClusters(const unsigned char* clustered,

+    if (seed >= point_count) return;

+    double* best_dist = reinterpret_cast<double*>(shared_storage);

// Calculate Euclidean distance between two points
__host__ __device__ inline double distance(const Point& p1, const Point& p2) {
    double dx = p1.x - p2.x;
    double dy = p1.y - p2.y;
    return std::sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);
}

__global__ void buildDistanceMatrix(const Point* points, double* distances, const int point_count) {
    const size_t index = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
    const size_t total = static_cast<size_t>(point_count) * point_count;
    if (index >= total) return;
    const int candidate = static_cast<int>(index / point_count);
    const int member = static_cast<int>(index % point_count);
    distances[index] = distance(points[candidate], points[member]);
}

// One block constructs one candidate cluster. Threads scan candidates in parallel;
// the shared reduction uses (distance, point index), exactly matching the original
// strict-improvement scan and therefore retaining deterministic tie breaking.
__global__ void buildCandidateClusters(const unsigned char* clustered,
                                       unsigned char* candidate_members, int* cardinalities,
                                       const double* distances,
                                       const double threshold, const int point_count) {
    const int seed = static_cast<int>(blockIdx.x);
    if (seed >= point_count) return;

    extern __shared__ unsigned char shared_storage[];
    double* best_dist = reinterpret_cast<double*>(shared_storage);
    int* best_index = reinterpret_cast<int*>(best_dist + blockDim.x);
    unsigned char* members = candidate_members + static_cast<size_t>(seed) * point_count;

    if (clustered[seed]) {
        if (threadIdx.x == 0) cardinalities[seed] = 0;
        return;
    }

    if (threadIdx.x == 0) {
        for (int i = 0; i < point_count; ++i) members[i] = 0;
        members[seed] = 1;
        cardinalities[seed] = 1;
    }
    __syncthreads();

    for (;;) {
        double local_dist = 1.7976931348623157e+308;
        int local_index = point_count;

        for (int candidate = threadIdx.x; candidate < point_count; candidate += blockDim.x) {
            if (clustered[candidate] || members[candidate]) continue;

            double max_dist = 0.0;
            for (int member = 0; member < point_count; ++member) {
                if (members[member]) {
                    max_dist = fmax(max_dist, distances[static_cast<size_t>(candidate) * point_count + member]);
                }
            }

            if (max_dist < threshold &&
                (max_dist < local_dist || (max_dist == local_dist && candidate < local_index))) {
                local_dist = max_dist;
                local_index = candidate;
            }
        }

        best_dist[threadIdx.x] = local_dist;
        best_index[threadIdx.x] = local_index;
        __syncthreads();

        for (int stride = blockDim.x / 2; stride > 0; stride >>= 1) {
            if (threadIdx.x < stride) {
                const double other_dist = best_dist[threadIdx.x + stride];
                const int other_index = best_index[threadIdx.x + stride];
                if (other_dist < best_dist[threadIdx.x] ||
                    (other_dist == best_dist[threadIdx.x] && other_index < best_index[threadIdx.x])) {
                    best_dist[threadIdx.x] = other_dist;
                    best_index[threadIdx.x] = other_index;
                }
            }
            __syncthreads();
        }

        if (threadIdx.x == 0) {
            const int selected = best_index[0];
    
    Point* device_points = nullptr;
    unsigned char* device_clustered = nullptr;
    unsigned char* device_candidate_members = nullptr;
    int* device_cardinalities = nullptr;
    double* device_distances = nullptr;
    cudaCheck(cudaMalloc(&device_points, sizeof(Point) * N), "allocating points");
    cudaCheck(cudaMalloc(&device_clustered, sizeof(unsigned char) * N), "allocating clustered flags");
    cudaCheck(cudaMalloc(&device_candidate_members, sizeof(unsigned char) * static_cast<size_t>(N) * N),
              "allocating candidate clusters");
    cudaCheck(cudaMalloc(&device_cardinalities, sizeof(int) * N), "allocating cardinalities");
    cudaCheck(cudaMalloc(&device_distances, sizeof(double) * static_cast<size_t>(N) * N),
              "allocating distance matrix");

    std::vector<unsigned char> clustered_bytes(N, 0);
    cudaCheck(cudaMemcpy(device_points, points.data(), sizeof(Point) * N, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice),
              "copying points");
    constexpr int distance_threads = 256;
    const size_t distance_elements = static_cast<size_t>(N) * N;
    buildDistanceMatrix<<<static_cast<unsigned int>((distance_elements + distance_threads - 1) /
                                                     distance_threads), distance_threads>>>(
        device_points, device_distances, N);
    cudaCheck(cudaGetLastError(), "launching distance-matrix kernel");
    cudaCheck(cudaDeviceSynchronize(), "building distance matrix");

    // Initialize unclustered indices. Their order is significant for ties.
    for (int i = 0; i < N; ++i) {
        unclustered_indices.push_back(i);
    }
    
    // Main clustering loop
    while (!unclustered_indices.empty()) {
        for (int i = 0; i < N; ++i) clustered_bytes[i] = clustered[i] ? 1 : 0;
        cudaCheck(cudaMemcpy(device_clustered, clustered_bytes.data(), sizeof(unsigned char) * N,
                             cudaMemcpyHostToDevice), "copying clustered flags");

        constexpr int threads_per_block = 256;
        const size_t shared_bytes = threads_per_block * (sizeof(double) + sizeof(int));
        buildCandidateClusters<<<N, threads_per_block, shared_bytes>>>(
            device_clustered, device_candidate_members, device_cardinalities,
            device_distances,
            threshold, N);
        cudaCheck(cudaGetLastError(), "launching candidate-cluster kernel");
        cudaCheck(cudaDeviceSynchronize(), "building candidate clusters");

        std::vector<int> cardinalities(N, 0);
        cudaCheck(cudaMemcpy(cardinalities.data(), device_cardinalities, sizeof(int) * N,
                             cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost), "copying cardinalities");

        int max_cardinality = -1;
        int best_seed = -1;
        for (size_t i = 0; i < unclustered_indices.size(); ++i) {
            const int seed = unclustered_indices[i];
            const int cardinality = cardinalities[seed];
            if (cardinality > max_cardinality) {
                max_cardinality = cardinality;
                best_seed = seed;
            }
        }
        
        // If we found a cluster, add it
        if (best_seed >= 0 && max_cardinality > 0) {
            std::vector<unsigned char> best_members(N, 0);
            cudaCheck(cudaMemcpy(best_members.data(), device_candidate_members +
                                 static_cast<size_t>(best_seed) * N, sizeof(unsigned char) * N,
                                 cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost), "copying selected cluster");
            Cluster cluster;
            cluster.seed_point = best_seed;
            cluster.members.reserve(max_cardinality);
            for (int i = 0; i < N; ++i) {
                if (best_members[i]) cluster.members.push_back(i);
            }
            clusters.push_back(cluster);
            
            // Mark all members as clustered
            for (const int member : cluster.members) {

+__global__ void buildDistanceMatrix(const Point* points, double* distances, const int point_count) {
+    const size_t index = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    const size_t total = static_cast<size_t>(point_count) * point_count;
+    if (index >= total) return;
+    const int candidate = static_cast<int>(index / point_count);
+    const int member = static_cast<int>(index % point_count);
+    distances[index] = distance(points[candidate], points[member]);

+// One block constructs one candidate cluster. Threads scan candidates in parallel;
+// the shared reduction uses (distance, point index), exactly matching the original
+// strict-improvement scan and therefore retaining deterministic tie breaking.
+__global__ void buildCandidateClusters(const unsigned char* clustered,
+                                       unsigned char* candidate_members, int* cardinalities,
+                                       const double* distances,
+                                       const double threshold, const int point_count) {

+    if (seed >= point_count) return;
+

+    double* best_dist = reinterpret_cast<double*>(shared_storage);

+        members[seed] = 1;
+        cardinalities[seed] = 1;

+    __syncthreads();
+

+

+            for (int member = 0; member < point_count; ++member) {

+                    max_dist = fmax(max_dist, distances[static_cast<size_t>(candidate) * point_count + member]);

 // Calculate Euclidean distance between two points
-inline double distance(const Point& p1, const Point& p2) {
+__host__ __device__ inline double distance(const Point& p1, const Point& p2) {
     double dx = p1.x - p2.x;
     double dy = p1.y - p2.y;
     return std::sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);
 }
 
-// Find the closest unclustered point to the current cluster that maintains diameter < threshold
-// Returns -1 if no such point exists
-int findClosestPoint(const std::vector<int>& cluster_members,

-

-    for (int candidate = 0; candidate < point_count; ++candidate) {
-        // Skip if already clustered or already in this cluster
-        if (clustered[candidate] || in_cluster[candidate]) continue;
-        
-        // Calculate the maximum distance from candidate to all cluster members
-        double max_dist = 0.0;
-        for (size_t i = 0; i < cluster_members.size(); ++i) {
-            const int member = cluster_members[i];
